### `assets/js/flows-ticker-movelab.js`, `tests/flows-movelab-render.mjs`

The Move lab on the ticker page, for every member. `tickerPage` always declares `<i hidden data-defer="movelab" id="ftMoveLab">`; `flows-ticker.js` adds `buildMoveLab`, which makes the `m-movelab` module for a card that is not an index or a fund and asks for the group with `FlowsUI.defer("movelab", {when: "visible"})`, handing the file the card, the card-x record, the card's spot and its daily closes. The file adds `FlowsUI.moveLab` (`mount`, and the pure `model`, `inputOf`, `rowOf`, `returnsOf`, `counted`, `frequency` and `fmt` the suite reads) and reads `GET /api/flows/universe?cols=ed,f2,f3,sig,t` once, only when the module nears the viewport. Three blocks. A size ladder of 1, 2, 3 and 4 times the name's typical daily move (`sig`, a fraction the nightly run publishes at scale 1e5; a figure of a quarter of the price or more is refused as a unit slip), each size with the price after a rise and after a fall (`spot x exp(+/- k x sig)`, to the cent, the log move the counts use, with its simple percent beside it), how many of the last 252 sessions moved that far (`f2` and `f3` for two and three times, report days left out; the card's own closes for one and four times, report days kept in, scaled to 252 when fewer returns are held and refused under 200) and how rare that is, in the sentence `frequencyText` writes, floor of 1 in 250 and an interval from five hits included, never a model probability. The options market's own implied move to the nearest end-of-week expiry from `panels.pricedMove` (`movePerc`, `low`, `high`), printed as the market's number and not a forecast. The earnings report, tagged event-contaminated when it is 21 sessions or fewer away (the card's `earnings.next.sessions`, else the universe's `ed`), with the market's report move. An absent input is a dash and its reason (no column, no row, too little history, no quote), never a zero, and a failed universe read offers Retry. A closing line says what the lab is not. No vendor call, no model call, no new global, no statistics jargon in its copy. `tests/flows-movelab-render.mjs` (Chromium, `page.route`, no server) holds every printed figure to an exact integer computation, `frequencyText` for all 253 counts and `sigmaRef`'s returns for the card counts, then the absent states, keyboard, touch hit areas, contrast, reduced motion and the 320 to 1440 px layout, and fails the planted wrong builds (the typical day taken as a percent, up and down swapped, the floor missing, and fifteen more).

The Market page's deferred chunk (F-08 part c), built the way Home's and Flow's are. `flows-market.js` keeps the head, the Tide, Breadth and Tape, the three index ETFs, Sector tides, the stale pill, the pulse stamp and every data read; the painters of Momentum, Extremes, Against the tape, the six pulse modules (Volume, Open interest, Net impact, Insiders, Dark pool, Seasonality), Advancers, Expiry, Volatility, Radar and Groups are in this file, declared as `<i hidden data-defer="market-below">` and asked for with `FlowsUI.defer("market-below", {when: "visible", el: #mkSectorsCard})`. ### `assets/js/flows-net.js`, `assets/css/flows-net.css`

The flow network, attached as `FlowsUI.net` (`model`, `mount`, `of`) and loaded only by the unusual page, whose hero it is. `model(rows, o)` is pure: each flagged window's premium is split by the vendor's ask-side and bid-side attribution (the rest, left between the quotes or never split, is Unattributed) and routed sector first (the nightly universe's, each real sector its own node ranked by premium and never folded into Other, then the Index options and Index funds buckets, then Other, No sector, Sector unread and Sector pending last; folded only past fourteen groups), then side (calls and puts on the ask or bid side, unsided), name (the largest ten, fewer on a narrow screen, and Other names), then lean (calls at ask and puts at bid bullish, calls at bid and puts at ask bearish, by convention) or expiry horizon (calendar days from the window's own Eastern date), so every layer, every hop and every node's outgoing edges sum to the same premium. Every layer is ranked by premium, largest at the top (rank numerals on the names), with the residual buckets last after a gap 1.6 times the normal one whatever their size (Other or Other names, then No sector, Sector unread, Sector pending; No expiry in the Expiry output). `mount(host)` draws v3 on Canvas 2D, laid out to `programme/NETWORK-DIRECTION.md`: money is the shape, a sphere's area is its premium (r = 6 + 28 sqrt(v / the largest node) px at 1440, never under 9) and a band's width is its premium (0.75 px per $1M, floor 1.25 px; the scale tightens as 56 px over the largest node so its bands never outgrow its sphere above a $75M node), one scale for every node and band and a legend row under the scene that draws three reference circles and three band swatches on that same scale; bands are filled Sankey bands stacked at each node in rank order so a node's outgoing widths add to its total, screen-blended in the lean colours (#2ee37a, #ff5a4e, #4ea2ff; Other and No sector in slate #6f7d99); spheres are lit sprites with a specular highlight and a rim, a halo that grows with premium, fog by depth and a soft shadow on a perspective floor grid; four glass planes stand behind the layers on a shallow arc. The scene is laid out in rest screen pixels (four columns at equal spacing centred on the midline, every stack centred with equal gaps, chips 22 px tall (32 px on two lines when the side chips between their neighbours would not fit on one) on one x edge per column, all four headers carrying the same total, controls above and the legend below, nothing else inside the scene) and lifted into world space with the inverse of the rest camera, so the symmetry holds to the pixel at rest and the Ambient sway (8 degrees over 40 s, pitch fixed at 11, reduced at layout to the widest amplitude at which no chip meets a sphere, a chip or a header, sampled at sixteen phases with the spheres at their 1.03 breathing maximum) supplies the depth. Below 640 px the layers become rows (ranks left to right, chips under their spheres in as many tiers as keep them apart) on a 0.62 scale; between 640 and 1100 px the scale is 0.85 or 0.72 and the chips two lines when one line would not clear the next column. Motion has two classes, and the loop runs only while something moves. Live (every viewer): a window that arrives while the page is open flares its name (a 1.2 s ring) and lights its path, a band carries pulses only for premium that arrived in the last five minutes (one to four by that band's share of the busiest band's arrivals, zero when nothing arrived), ranks glide when new data reorders them, and the camera moves only while the reader drags it plus inertia that is over inside 8 s; at rest the paint census is zero. Ambient (the Ambient button, the `ambient` tag and a legend key reading `ambient motion, not data`): pulses along every band at 7 s down to 2.4 s a lap and one to four by premium, spheres breathing 1.00 to 1.03 in rank-staggered phases, and the yaw sway; it opens off for everyone and sits still until the reader presses Ambient, the reader's own click remembered in a first-party preference cookie `fn_ambient` (Path `/flows`, one year, the pattern of `flows_gate`; `localStorage` is reserved for `IEWTStorage`) and winning over both; `ambient:on` and `ambient:off` bubble from the host. No pass uses the `lighter` composite operation, and pulses are computed from the loop's clock with no per-frame allocation (batched by width into at most twenty strokes). hover, focus or a table row lights a subgraph and dims the rest, with a readout of the node's figure and share at the top right; Freeze stops the pulses, breathing, flares and sway in both classes (its label swaps to Resume and it carries no `aria-pressed`, so a screen reader hears one state; it is hidden under reduced motion, which draws one still frame). Below 640 px the four layer headers are drawn as dark chips with no band crossing them, and a hover readout always clears the headers. When the web font arrives after the first draw (`document.fonts.ready` and `loadingdone`, debounced to a frame and acted on only when the measured width of a reference string changed) the labels are measured again and the layout rebuilt, with keyboard focus kept. Under 540 px of module width the legend is two groups, each a title row over one row of three items, and the paths table rebalances its columns so no header truncates at 320 px. A pointer or horizontal touch drag orbits the camera (yaw within 55 degrees, 30 on a phone, pitch within 22, with inertia) but never past the angle at which a chip would meet a sphere, a chip or a header (`fits`, 6 bisections), so at 1440 it stops near 27 to 36 degrees; a double click or tap, R or 0 on a node, or Recentre returns it. The painter's order is floor, shadows, planes, bands, pulses, headers, spheres back to front, labels, and it stops when the tab is hidden or the canvas is off screen. `destroy()` cancels the loop, aborts the universe fetch, disconnects its observers and removes its listeners. The node hit targets (one roving tab stop after Freeze and Recentre, arrow keys between nodes, names linking to the ticker page), the canvas's spoken summary and the table of the 24 largest paths are its accessible half. It reads `/api/flows/universe` once for sectors and nothing else; the unusual controller hands it the alerts it already holds.

### `assets/js/flows-export.js`, `tests/flows-export.mjs`

The export menu, attached to `FlowsUI` the way `flows-fresh.js` does (a re-frozen copy), loaded only on the boards (`/flows/long/`, `/flows/short/`, `/flows/watch/`) and the desk, before their controllers. `FlowsUI.csv` writes RFC 4180 with CRLF, a byte order mark, the unit in each header and two padded footer rows (source and as-of, then the export instant, printed as the TRT stamp with the same instant as an ISO 8601 string at +03:00 in brackets and no UTC instant); a string cell that starts with `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, tab or CR gets a leading apostrophe, a finite number is written bare, and null, undefined, NaN and Infinity are empty. The menu offers CSV for the view as the reader sees it (sort and filter applied) and SVG and PNG of the board map and the desk frontier, serialised with computed colours so no custom property is left unresolved. Exports are limited to the site's own scores, model outputs and aggregates (OD-30): the board's last price and day change, and the desk's quoted premium, are not columns. The suite is Chromium with `page.route` stubs, no server, and reads each download back through an independent CSV parser.

### `assets/js/flows-status.js`, `assets/js/flows-status-latency.js`, `assets/js/flows-status-capacity.js`, `assets/js/flows-status-coverage.js`, `assets/css/flows-status.css`, `tests/flows-status-render.mjs`, `tests/lib/status-world.mjs`

The owner-only Status and Capacity page, `/flows/status/` (`statusPage`, a row in `server/routes/flows-pages.js` marked `ownerOnly`: a signed-in member who is not an owner gets the unknown-page 404, an anonymous visitor the sign-in page, and no switch opens it to members). It fits one screen with no page scroll at 1440x820, 1024x768 and 390x844: a title row, a readiness line (its blockers behind a button and a dialog), four slim tiles, a tablist of four tabs (Health, Capacity, Latency, Coverage; `FlowsUI.segmented` with arrow, Home and End keys, `aria-controls` and `aria-labelledby` to four tabpanels, the address `#view=` written with `replaceState` and read on load and on `hashchange`, the old `usage` landing on Capacity) over a grid of cards. Every card is a summary that fits its box: a state line, one or two gauges or a compact table whose rows are trimmed to the height the card has by `fit` (a `data-what` table loses trailing rows until nothing overflows and says `+N more <what> in Details`), re-run on resize; a Details button opens a native `dialog` that holds the full module, painted by the same code (the long tables, the stage ledger, the plan lines, the switches), so nothing is dropped, only moved. Below 641 px one card shows at a time, chosen by a second segmented switch (`aria-controls` the card). The sidebar's Operations group, shown to owners on every Flows page by `ownerNav`, links Status and Latency. Reads, each polled only for the tab that shows it and only while the page is visible (one timer, no read and no timer while hidden, one immediate read on return, the interval doubled after an error up to five minutes): `/api/rt/status` (30 s, 10 s on Latency), `/api/flows/ops` (60 s), `/api/flows/ops?part=ai` (once, Capacity and Latency), `/api/flows/ops?part=coverage` (5 min, Coverage), `/api/flows/lk?k=heartbeat` (60 s), `/api/flows/lk?k=strips:series` (5 min, Latency), `/api/flows/meta`, `/api/flows/ready` and `/api/flows/ai-usage` (5 min). It makes no vendor call and no model call, adds no payload and no global (`FlowsUI.statusKit` is a property). Health: the engines (nightly, Tier 1, focus, Tier 2, worst stale key, the GitHub dispatch refusal), the rail (each topic's lag from `lagMs` for stamped topics and `itemLagMs` for flagged alerts and news), the nightly run with its slowest stages, the read-only switches. Capacity: the vendor key's calls today (a reading counts as today's only while its vendor window, from the ops view, holds the page's clock, and the turn of that window is printed in TRT with the dashboard it is stated on) and the rail's minute against their lines, the model neurons and calls against the enforced 30,000 cap with the free allowance and the dollars beyond it, the platform meters (D1, Durable Objects, Workers requests: the allowance and a dash for use with its reason) and the cron ticks from the ledger, and the limits enforced. Latency: feed p50 and p95, the Tier 2 quote age per pass as a chart whose numbers are in a dialog, the stored feeds worst first, the timing of the engines, the socket and this browser. Coverage: every declared nightly and shared key by its size against the 100 KiB budget, its session against the nightly's, and the keys missing, then the nightly's cards built, failed and skipped. Every time is written `HH:MM TRT` (`HH:MM:SS TRT` for reads) by a local formatter that adds three hours (Türkiye has no daylight time), with the date only when it is not today, and no other zone is named. A source that cannot be read, or a value it does not carry, is a dash with its reason, never zero; what cannot be measured on the site (D1 rows, Durable Object use, Workers requests, option prints, the vendor's per-minute counter, the card rows' count) says so. Nothing is invented: there is no governor, no toggle and no projection. The panels are the deferred group `status-panels` (`flows-chart.js`, `flows-status-latency.js`, `flows-status-capacity.js`, `flows-status-coverage.js`, requested the first time a tab other than Health opens). `tests/lib/status-world.mjs` builds the fixtures, the Worker's real `opsBody` among them, and serves the page. The suite is Chromium with `page.route` stubs, no server: the routes each tab asks and no others, no page scroll, no clipped card and no horizontal scroll for four tabs at three viewports, every printed figure against its fixture, the dialogs, the phone's card switch, nulls and failures as dashes with reasons, TRT only, the refresh census over a paused clock, hidden-tab silence, error backoff, axe, and planted wrong builds (an unfitted card, a lost note, a frame lag for alerts, an Eastern clock, a fixed source count, the free allowance as the cap, a missing key as zero bytes, a share against the wrong budget, a zero for an absent figure) that must each fail.

### `docs/flows-design-system.md`, `tests/flows-kit-render.mjs`

The Flows design system v2: the surface, elevation, focus, density, data-type and sequential tokens and the `.ds-` kit (module, state chip, button, row, signed figure) that sit in `assets/css/flows.css`, the fourteen module states (`loading`, `quiet`, `partial`, `pending`, `stale`, `withheld`, `unavailable`, `signedOut`, `quota`, `plan`, `held`, `live`, `fresh`, `closed`), the colour-vision rule (a pair under CIEDE2000 20 in any of three simulations carries a printed sign and a shape) and the forced-colors rules. Every added rule is a `:root` property or a `.ds-` selector, so no page changes until it adopts a class. The test serves the shipped stylesheets from disk on a fake origin and renders the kit at 320, 390, 768 and 1440 px: no overflow, text at 4.5:1 over its composited surface, a 44 px hit area by `elementFromPoint`, visible focus by Tab, the named states, forced colors, reduced motion and a coarse pointer; Chromium, no server.

### `tests/flows-glass-render.mjs`, `tests/lib/glass-world.mjs`

The liquid-glass contrast suite (design v3, Lane 1), the authority on the contrast budget of `docs/flows-design-system.md` (Materials v3). `tests/lib/glass-world.mjs` serves Home (`openHome`), Market (`openMarket` with the dry emit), the long board and the ticker (the emit's board, card, card-x and history rows with a deterministic reading) on `https://example.test` from `page.route` with no server, applies a mode and clears it again with `clearMode` so one page serves all four (`prefers-reduced-transparency: reduce` through CDP, `prefers-contrast: more` and `forced-colors: active` through `emulateMedia`, or an injected stylesheet with the `@supports not` token values), and measures: `measureText` makes every ink transparent (and the `background-clip: text` gradient of a `.ui-num` with it), screenshots the viewport at device scale 1, takes the brightest pixel under each text run's own line boxes (clipped by its scrolling ancestors; if any box has moved by more than 0.75 px between collecting it and the screenshot, because a deferred module mounted or a font arrived, the step is collected again, up to six times, rather than judged on the wrong pixels), composites the computed colour with its alpha and opacity chain over it and requires 4.5:1, or 3:1 at 24 px and from 18.66 px at weight 600, with every stop of a gradient ink tested; `measureMaterial` puts a `.ui-card` carrying every ink of the budget table (`--label-1`, `--label-2`, `--label-3`, `--down`, `--accent-ink`, `--up` and the ice, up and down `.ui-num` gradients) in the top band and the body on each radial centre of `--ground` with the page hidden beneath, so the material is measured over the bare aurora. `MUTANTS` holds the six planted wrong builds. `openLensRig` serves the real `sidePage` markup and stylesheets with no script on a fake origin and mounts a segmented control (the real `.ui-seg` and `.ui-seg-knob` rules) over a hairline grid, and `measureLens` screenshots the knob with the lens off and on (a control filter with a flat displacement map first proves the engine can bend a backdrop) and reads how far each grid line moved, to a tenth of a pixel; `lensMutants` holds the seven planted lens builds (a flat map, a map that pushes the middle, a map gentle at the rim, a missing filter, a missing `@supports` gate, a lens kept under reduced transparency and under forced colours). The suite runs the four pages at 1920x1080, 1440x820 and 390x844, the material probe at the three sizes (the blue pool's band and body held to the budget's rgb figures), the four fallback modes on every page at 1440x820 (opaque tokens, a flat sheen, a solid rim, no blur; `CanvasText` borders, hidden rims and a `Highlight` knob under forced colours), the floating sidebar, the tile layers, the glass bar controls, the segmented knob and the sidebar's site switcher, the refraction of four surfaces (one `#fx-lens` filter on each shell page right after the glyph sprite, `lensMap()` rebuilt from the markup, the `@supports` gate, the drops under reduced transparency, high contrast and forced colours, only the four named surfaces reading `--glass-lens`, and on the grid at least 2 px of displacement at the knob's rim and under 0.5 px at its centre, recorded as a fallback where the engine cannot bend a backdrop), reduced motion, a sweep of every shell page for a scrolling tile that still draws a rim (`openStaticShell`, `scrollRims`), the mask order and the vendor-prefix pairs in the source, and requires each planted wrong build (`--label-3` at 0.52 with the sheen at 0.065, `--mat-regular` at 0.50, the aurora alphas doubled, the reduced-transparency additions deleted, the rim left visible under forced colours, a sheen of 0.08 reaching 60%) and each of the seven lens builds to fail. Text inside `svg` (chart axis and level labels) is not measured: it sits over the chart's own marks, not over the glass, so the pixel under it says nothing about the material.

### `assets/js/flows-chart.js`

The Flows chart library and kernel (`FlowsUI.chart`: `mount`, `line`, `bars`, `diverging`, `heatmap`, `sparkline`, `gauge`, `payoff` and the primitives under them, plus the kernel: `kind(name, impl)` and `plot(host, spec)` over a closure `Map` registry (a duplicate name throws and the frozen object is never mutated), `scale` (linear, log, sqrt, band, session with closed hours compressed), `ticks` (labels through `F.unit` by kind name; a log domain with no positive part has none and one under a decade falls back to nice ticks), a two-dimensional `scrub` for bubble and scatter charts, a hidden description written from a kind's `data()` (the last value with its unit and time, then the low and the high), an optional table view and a provenance footer). The v1 calls are adapters over the same registry and render the same marks, held by node and attribute snapshots in `tests/fixtures-chart-kernel.json`. `dateTicks`, `fx1`, `tw` and `heightFor` are exported so that a controller keeps no copy of them (the ticker kept five), and `scrub` takes `ys`, `rs`, `yw` and `reach` for a chart whose marks are found in two dimensions, `first` to start the keyboard at the first mark, `xh` for the crosshair's opacity, `ariaHint` for the sentence after the label and, from a readout, `say` for the words announced. Split out of `flows-ui.js`. It re-publishes a new frozen `FlowsUI` with `chart` added, the way `flows-fresh.js` and `flows-rt.js` do, and `shared/flows-pages.js` emits it directly after `flows-ui.js` on the ten chart pages only (overview, the long and short boards, watch, desk, market, track, unusual, ticker, history, strategy); events, political, Ask and login never download it, so `FlowsUI.chart` is absent there. It duplicates the eight helpers `flows-ui.js` does not export (`paint`, `isoDay`, `AH`, `nextId`, `RAF`, `MON`, `spoken`, `moving`) rather than widening the frozen surface. Script order is load-bearing: a script that captured `window.FlowsUI` between the two would lose `chart`; `tests/flows-weight.mjs` and the worker and ticker contracts hold the order.

### `assets/js/flows-skyline.js`, `tests/flows-skyline-render.mjs`

The move skyline (F-95, placement 3) on the Market page. `FLOWS_SCENES` (`wrangler.toml` [vars], shipped `on`) is `on`, `owner` or off: the Worker passes `scenes` to `marketPage` when the switch is `on`, or is `owner` and the member is an owner (`FLOWS_RT_USERS`), and the page then emits the `mkSkylineCard` module and the `skyline` defer group, so a member's HTML carries neither. `flows-market.js` hands the universe it already reads to `FlowsUI.skyline.mount` when the module is within 200 px of the viewport; no new request or payload. `model(u)` is pure: `IV-days = chg / (iv30p / sqrt(252))` per priced name (the IV-days lens of `shared/flows-moves0.js`), prisms for the names at or beyond 2 IV-days, at most 40, a name with earnings inside 21 sessions marked as an event. A toggle (`FlowsUI.segmented`, arrow keys) sets what height shows. IV-days: the move itself, not a probability, no selection line, no error rate measured. How often: the count of days over the last year for that name as far out as the rung its move reached, read from the universe's `sig`, `f2` and `f3` (`sigmaProfile` through `buildUniverse`; a null cell is absence): 3x when the absolute change over `sig` is at least 3, 2x from 2, none under 2. N is 252 over the count, floored at 250 as `rarityText` does (a zero count or N over 250 is `rarer than 1 in 250`, drawn at the floor under a line above the prism), and height is `log10(N) / log10(250)` of the span on a labelled log axis ticked 1 in 2, 5, 10, 25, 50, 100 and the floor. A name with no scale, under 2 typical days, or with earnings inside 21 sessions is not drawn there and is named in a note; the option is off when none has a count. No p and no sigma wording; `assets/js` imports nothing, so the browser carries its own copy of the reader rule and the test holds every sentence equal to `rarityText`. Direction is on the colour channel (`--up-mark`, `--down-mark`) and in a triangle and sign in the tables; an event prism is paler with a dashed top. The three tallest in the active encoding (largest IV-days, or rarest) are called out with a box in the right margin (triangle, ticker, signed IV-days, the reader-rule sentence or the reason there is none): their prisms sit on the back edge, where nothing stands behind them, and each leader rises to its own lane above the skyline and runs to its stacked box, so none crosses a prism or another leader. Canvas 2D, a 2:1 dimetric projection, no label on any other prism; from 640 px of module width, below that the table of the three and the table of the names (a How often column, sorted by the active encoding) stand in. `tests/flows-skyline-render.mjs` (Chromium, `page.route`, no server) holds heights to IV-days and to `log10(N)` against independent sums and `rarityText`, the ticks, the toggle by key and click, the callouts and `labelProblems` at 640 to 1440 px and on 40 prisms, the absence note, a `buildUniverse` payload read back, pixel colour and tokens, the empty and unpublished states, no overflow at 320, 390 and 1440, and seventeen planted wrong builds.

### `assets/js/flows-moves.js`, `assets/css/flows-moves.css`, `tests/flows-moves-render.mjs`, `tests/lib/moves-fixture.mjs`

The Moves page (`/flows/moves/`, F-57), a one-screen field of every name in the nightly universe: `universe?cols=t,sec,chg,iv30p,sig,f2,f3,ed,mcap` (plus the live roster's `px` strips when their session is newer). Across is the move in the name's own typical days (`chg / sig`, `sig` the nightly's typical daily move), up is the move as a multiple of the implied day (`abs(chg) / (iv30p / sqrt(252))`, the prior close's 30-day IV; the one page-side division, with `shared/flows-moves0.js` as its oracle). Each dot is a triangle for a riser, an inverted triangle for a faller and a circle for a flat name, its area the market cap (square root scale, 2.5 px floor, the smallest size for a name with no cap), ringed when earnings are within 21 sessions; SPY and QQQ are fixed squares kept whatever the filter; the axes run to the 99.5th percentile of the drawn names (at least 3, capped at 12 and 10) and a name past them is drawn at the edge and counted in the foot. Dashed lines at 2 and 3 typical days and at 1 times implied are reference marks, not a test; no boundary curve exists; the significance test is the one in `shared/flows-moves-sig.js`, which the page carries a copy of and the drill panel and the foot show. Filters (Up, Down, Earnings week, a sector, Clear) fade the other dots and re-rank the lists; Freeze holds the read, prints the time in TRT and applies the held one on Resume; the order also holds while the pointer or focus is in the lists. Large now ranks names outside the earnings window by IV-days; Earnings within 21 sessions ranks the others apart; rows show a short rarity chip and the whole sentence in the title and the drill panel, only through the `rarityText` rules of `shared/flows-targets.js` (a count of 252 from `f2` or `f3`, the 1-in-250 floor, never a model probability) and only from 2 typical days. Show more opens a dialog sized to the viewport. The drill panel gives the move against its typical and implied day, how often, the card's last 21 closes and context (conviction, IV rank, dealer book, latest flow) read lazily from `GET /api/flows/card?t=` after first paint with a Retry, and the test's sentence or the reason for none; the info dialog lists what is not built (parts of the move, flow clusters, replay). One screen at 1440x820, 1024x768 and 390x844 with no scroll and no inner scroll; a phone switches Field, Lists and Detail; arrow keys move between the sixty largest dots and the pins, Enter opens the ticker page, a tap selects the nearest dot within 22 px. Copy is held to fourteen words a sentence and none of unusual, alert, screen, rare, sigma or radar. `assets/js` imports nothing, so the page carries its own copy of the reader rule and the suite holds every sentence equal to `rarityText` for all 253 counts at both levels. No model, no vendor call, no new payload, no new global (`__MovesTest` exists only where there is no document). Gated by `FLOWS_MOVES` in `wrangler.toml` (shipped `on`; `owner` when unset, `on`, `off`; anything else fails closed for a member, and a refused request is the unknown-page 404). The suite is Chromium with `page.route` stubs, a synthetic universe (`tests/lib/moves-fixture.mjs`, no vendor data) and no server: zero scroll and nothing clipped in every panel, filter and dialog at three sizes, each dot's position, radius, shape and ring against an independent computation, lists, filters, Freeze, the live roster, the drill panel, a failed card and its Retry, the keyboard and a tap, and twenty-one planted wrong builds that each fail.

### `shared/flows-flow-net.js`, `assets/js/flows-ticker-network.js`, `assets/css/flows-ticker-network.css`, `tests/flows-flow-net-render.mjs`

The ticker's Flow network, the dock's last module (its button used to say it was not built for the name). `tickerPage` declares `<i hidden data-defer="network" id="ftNetwork">` with the group file and its own sheet (`data-css`, loaded by the file itself, so the eager sheet and the eager weight of the page do not move); `flows-ticker.js` adds `buildNetwork`, which makes the `m-network` module for every card and asks for the group when it nears the viewport (in the dock's sheet or in the one-page grid). It is built from the name's own card-x `alerts` record and nothing else: no new request, vendor call or payload. The 30 largest flagged windows of the session (`dots`: premium, call or put, strike, expiry, ask-side share, sweep, all-opening) are split into four columns that each add to the same total and print shares that add to exactly 100 (largest remainder, so a larger node never prints a smaller share than a smaller one, and a positive node under half a percent prints as under 1%): side (calls and puts on the ask side by each window's own ask share, and the rest, which is bid, mid or unattributed and never a lean), expiry (same day, 1 to 7, 8 to 31, 32 and over calendar days, counted from the `YYYY-MM-DD` parts with `Date.UTC`, so no clock change moves a bucket), strike against the session spot (in the money, near or out of it by call and put sense; near is within a quarter of a standard deviation, `ln(K/S) / (sigma x sqrt(max(days, 1) / 365))`, with sigma read at the window's own expiry by linear interpolation of total variance between the card's term rows (held flat outside them), else the 30-day IV; with a spot and no volatility the band is a fixed 2.5% of the spot, measured as a difference so 97.5 and 102.5 on a spot of 100 are both near, and the nodes say so; with no spot the column is not drawn) and flags (sweep and all-opening, sweep, all-opening, neither, and flags not read: a missing flag never falls into neither). A window with no ask share is counted and put wholly in the rest, never coerced to the ask side; a window with an unplaceable price, side, strike or expiry (a date that is not a real calendar day included) is excluded from every column and counted; when none can be placed, or the windows carry no session date, the state is unavailable with that reason and never quiet. Coverage is premium over the name's flagged premium, never a window count: at three quarters or more the standard line, under it a warning that shares can differ by up to `100 x (1 - coverage)` points for the whole name, under half it says the largest windows only; windows adding to more than the name's premium, a session that is not the card's, a quiet or absent record and fewer than five windows are states with their reason (the last draws the table only). The map is chips with bands on one width scale (a band is a pair of adjacent nodes, which is not a path); the readout and the fill under each chip come from the selected node's own windows (the joint over the other columns), never chained through the bands; arrow keys move between chips, a table of the paths is the accessible twin, and under about 480 px of module width the table stands in and says why. `shared/flows-flow-net.js` is the oracle (`buildNet`, `readoutFor`, `moneyness`, `volFor`, `shares`); the page carries its own copy and `tests/flows-flow-net-render.mjs` holds the two equal over 600 random worlds and 3,000 random columns of shares beside hand-worked cases (moneyness at 0, 30 and 400 days, the March and November changes of clocks under `TZ=America/New_York`), loads the real ticker page against the dry-run emit at 1440, 1024 and 390 px, checks every chip's text, every band's endpoints and width scale, the selection, the keyboard, the table, the states and the coverage warnings, and fails planted wrong builds of the model and of the page (the card's ask share for the side column, a missing ask share not counted or read as zero, a missing flag read as neither, an unscaled or unfloored moneyness, calls and puts swapped, the nearer or the farther expiry's volatility, or an interpolation in volatility rather than total variance, coverage by window count, a local-time day count, shares rounded one by one or by cumulative sums, a window dropped from some columns only, a readout that ignores the selection, bands off their chips, a width scale that is not premium, and others).

### `shared/flows-moves-sig.js`, `tests/flows-moves-sig.mjs`

The Moves significance test. A leaf over `flows-stats.js` alone. For each name, `z = chg / sig` in the name's own typical days; below 2 typical days it is not tested but counted in the correction with p = 1; earnings within 21 sessions, no typical day and no published count are refused with a reason. Otherwise p is the add-one exceedance rate `(hits + 1) / 253` at the nearest level (`f2` from 2 to under 3 typical days, `f3` from 3), conservative for a move inside the level. Benjamini-Hochberg runs across every counted name at q = 0.10 and a name is beyond the usual when its adjusted p is at most q. The smallest p is 1 in 253, so few names pass except on a wide day. The page carries its own copy (`sigRun` in `__MovesTest`), the drill panel prints the sentence, p and adjusted p, the foot counts the flagged, and the info dialog states the limits (clustered market days inflate the test; a ranking aid, not a forecast). `tests/flows-moves-sig.mjs` holds the leaf to worked answers, an independent step-up reference on 300 random universes, the page's copy on 200, a seeded null universe's false-flag rate at or under 10%, power, the sentences, and 11 planted wrong builds.

### `assets/js/flows-political.js`, `assets/css/flows-political.css`, `tests/flows-political-render.mjs`

The Political page (`/flows/political/`), stock first and one screen with no page scroll at 1440x820, 1024x768 and 390x844. Rows are tickers read from the one `GET /api/flows/political` row; people appear only as initials of evidence. Left panel: tabs Accumulating, Reducing and Convergence (counts with funds hidden; `FUND_TICKERS` behind a toggle), a Map or List switch and a Dollars or Filers switch. The map is a squarified treemap whose tile area is the square root of disclosed dollars (bought plus sold band midpoints; `treemapWeight`) or filers plus a dollar tiebreak, at most 18 tiles (12 on a phone), each with a ring of the median lag over the window (`blindShare`) and a state from `netBounds` and `stateOf`: low bound = bought low minus twice the sold midpoint, high bound = bought high minus an eighth of it, firm accumulating only when the low bound is above zero and no band is open, and no name is ever firm reducing. Right panel: the newest filings that name a stock, with a Show all dialog of all of them. Bottom rail for the selected name: disclosed dollars with range, breadth and median lag, the traded-versus-filed strip with the typically-unfiled band, Flows context and evidence. Flows context is one lazy `GET /api/flows/card?t=` per selected name, debounced 220 ms and cached, reading conviction, IV rank, dealer gamma book and flow, the last net premium and the price since the newest buy; an absent card or field is an em dash with its reason and a failure offers Retry. Phones switch between Stocks, Filings and Detail. Arrow keys move between tiles by geometry, Enter opens the ticker page. Only tokens, no colour literal. `tests/flows-political-render.mjs` (Chromium, `page.route` stubs on a synthetic payload of the real shape, no server) holds the scroll, areas, states, rings, strip, keyboard, card states and copy to independent computations and fails ten planted wrong builds.

### `assets/js/flows-relations.js`, `tests/flows-relations-render.mjs`, `tests/fixtures-relations.json`, `tests/gen-relations-fixtures.py`

The Relations tab on the Market page (`FlowsUI.relations`: `mount`, `model`, `pearson`, `partial`, `fisher`), under the `FLOWS_SCENES` switch. It relates the names that carry the day's flagged option windows, the data the page already reads from `/api/flows/flowalerts` and nothing else: each window's premium (and its net bullish premium, calls on the ask side and puts on the bid side minus the reverse, by convention) is put into ten-minute bins from its own `spanStart`, the twelve names with the most flagged premium and at least four active bins are kept, a bin with no window is zero, and every pair gets Pearson r, the Fisher z 95% interval on the bin count, and r in each half of the window. A pair is drawn only when its interval excludes zero, the absolute r is at least 0.3 and both halves keep the sign (`stable`); there are three relations (`Active together`, `Lean together` and `Beyond the market`) shown one at a time. `Beyond the market` is the partial correlation of the Active series after each is regressed on the mean of the kept names' standardised Active series (per half too), with the Fisher interval on n - 4 (one conditioned variable); it removes the day's shared flow burst, not stock-price moves, and says so. The page says how many of the pairs would pass by chance (5% of those tested), that windows arrive in bursts so the interval is too narrow, and that it is not a forecast. Fewer than 40 windows, 20 bins or three names is `thin` with the counts and no drawing; a failed or unpublished feed says so with its reason. It is a ring by sector (each node its ticker and its sector, every one labelled) beside a list of the tested pairs with a Show all dialog; under 900 px the ring and the list are a switch. Not built: price-residual and price partial-correlation layers (they need daily closes for the universe, which no stored payload carries) and the sector ETF return correlation (the sectors payload holds a smoothed TRIX series, whose correlation would be spurious). `tests/gen-relations-fixtures.py` (numpy, scipy; versions and its own SHA-256 in the fixture) writes three seeded worlds (a factor world, a null world and a short one) with the expected r, interval, halves and verdict of every pair; `tests/flows-relations-render.mjs` (Chromium, no server) holds the module to them at 1e-9, the thin, quiet, failed and pending states, three viewports, and fifteen planted wrong builds.

### `assets/js/flows-net-map.js`, `assets/css/flows-net-map.css`, `tests/flows-net-map-render.mjs`

The Market page's flow network, condensed (`FlowsUI.netMap`: `mount`, `of`, `readout`), loaded as the `netmap` defer group after `flows-net.js` (its pure `FlowsUI.net.model` does the routing, so a premium is split, sectored and bucketed once, the way the Unusual page's scene does it). Four columns of chips on one screen, SVG not canvas: sector (every sector kept, index buckets and residuals last), side (calls and puts on the ask or bid side, unsided), name (the largest ten, fewer when the height asks, and Other names), and lean or expiry. Every chip carries its full label and its figure and share (`$41.3M · 25%`); a sector name is abbreviated only when it will not fit the chip, and then the full name is in the chip's title, accessible name and the readout. A chip is two lines whenever the height allows. Bands run between chips at a width that is the premium (one scale for every band, a legend swatch shows a round dollar figure on it), colour by dominant lean, and the chip's base carries the node's own bullish, unsided and bearish split. A persistent readout names the selected node's layer, its premium, its share of the total, where it comes from and where it goes, its windows and contracts, and the lean convention; at rest it is the largest name, a click, Enter or the arrow keys change it, and the selected paths are lit. Other and Other names say what they fold in and open a member list with each member's premium. The table twin lists every path by sector, side, name, lean or expiry, premium and share, top rows with Show all. Under 640 px one layer at a time is a switch and the legend is a button. `tests/flows-net-map-render.mjs` (Chromium, `page.route`, no server) audits every chip against independent sums over the same alert rows, its text against its width, chips and captions for overlap, the bands' widths, the readout, the legend and the table at 1440, 1024 and 390 px, and fails eleven planted wrong builds. The Unusual and Home scenes (`flows-net.js`) are unchanged.

The sector heat map (`paintSectorTides`, a squarified treemap in `flows-market.js`): one tile for every sector, each area its share of the weight the larger of the absolute net premium and 6% of the largest within 0.4 percentage points, tiles inside the map, covering it, none overlapping, most net-bought first, tone by sign, value equal to the sector's last net. The tape tiles' meters: the call and put split, the two put to call scales, the offer shares and the rank. Run at 1440 and 390 px, with ten planted wrong builds (a tile chart left visible on another tab, area without the floor, tiles most net-sold first, swapped breadth segments, a wrong meter scale, an upside-down split, a dropped path, the wrong tide leg, a flipped tile tone, a wrong dossier link) that each fail.

### `assets/js/flows-market-river.js`, `tests/flows-market-river-render.mjs`

The Market page's opening scene, the Session (`FlowsUI.river`: `model`, `layout`, `mount`, `of`, `sideOf`, `trtOf`), loaded as the `river` defer group into the first tab, `#view=session`, which is the page's default view. Depth is time: the open at the back, the last read at the front, under a now line stamped in TRT. The lanes across are the sectors of the names the vendor flagged today (the universe's sector, `Index funds` for SPY, QQQ, IWM and DIA, `No sector` for a name the universe lacks, `Sector unread` for every name when the universe has not published), ordered by flagged premium. Each flagged window of `/api/flows/flowalerts` is a lit sphere sprite (the one `flows-net.js` draws) at its `spanStart` minute in its name's lane, radius `0.03 + 0.12 sqrt(premium / largest)` in lane units so area follows premium on one scale; colour is the side by convention (a call with more ask than bid premium or a put with more bid green, bullish; the reverse red, bearish; no split or equal blue, unsided). The lane floor is tiled by the live breadth key's 5-minute steps and tinted by the sector's `net` at that step (the funds lane the sum of the ETF tides), slate with a dashed edge and a stated reason when the key is absent, for another session or missing the sector, never a zero. The market tide of `pulse` (calls up, puts down) stands on the left wall along the same minutes, only when it is the same session. Camera, fit, fog, floor grid, glass wall and label declutter follow `flows-ticker-surface.js` and `flows-net.js`; every colour is read from the page's tokens. No motion: a paint happens on mount, on data, on resize, on hover or focus, and never at rest. The accessible twin is a readout (`role=status`, the largest window at rest, the hovered or focused window or lane otherwise), a hit link over each of the 40 largest windows (one roving tab stop, arrows, Home, End, Escape; Enter opens the ticker page), a button over each lane header, a canvas description, and two tables (lanes with their tide; the largest windows with time in TRT, side, premium, expiry). A failed, pending or empty feed is a state with its reason. `tests/flows-market-river-render.mjs` (Chromium, `page.route`, no server) recomputes every lane sum, side, lane band, minute position, radius and screen projection from the fixture rows with its own camera, holds the tiles to the emitted breadth key and samples their pixels, checks labels for overlap, the TRT ticks, the now line, three viewports with no scroll, reduced motion, the keyboard, a zero paint census at rest, the frame cost against a reference canvas workload, the four states, and twelve planted wrong builds.

### `assets/js/flows-market-floor.js`, `tests/flows-market-floor-render.mjs`

The Market page's opening view, the Floor (`FlowsUI.floor`: `model`, `layout`, `mount`, `of`, `sideOf`, `valueOf`, `classify`), loaded as the `floor` defer group into the first tab, `#view=floor`, which is the page's default (the Session river is the second tab). A lit perspective floor on Canvas 2D with one glass pillar for each class of what the vendor flagged today, read from `/api/flows/flowalerts` and the universe's sectors and nothing else (no new request, payload or vendor call): the four index funds (SPY, QQQ, IWM, DIA), the eleven sector ETFs (XLK to XLRE), any flagged class of other funds (Bonds, Commodities, Volatility, Crypto, International, by a fixed ticker table) and single stocks grouped by the universe's sector (`No sector`, or one `Sector unread` tile when the universe has not published). Rows run from the back in that order, each headed by its group's flagged premium and share, so the question of whether funds, sector ETFs or stocks draw the most is answered at a glance. A height switch (`FlowsUI.segmented`: Premium, Volume, Net lean) sets pillar height on one scale across every class: the sum of flagged premium, the sum of the windows' contract counts (a window with no count adds nothing and the class says how many windows the figure comes from; a class with none is a dash, never a zero), or the absolute net lean (calls on the ask side and puts on the bid side minus the reverse, by convention). Colour is lean by convention, `--up-mark`, `--down-mark` or slate when the net is within 10 per cent of premium, deepening to full at 60 per cent; the white stripe along each base is the class's share of all flagged premium relative to the largest; a class with nothing flagged is a dashed slate tile, never a zero. Pillars are glass boxes (vertical gradient, specular strip on the lit face, rim, soft shadow sprite, glow by share) drawn far to near with fog by depth. A persistent readout (`role=status`) names the hovered or selected class with premium, share, volume, lean and its top three names; clicking or pressing Enter on a pillar drills a side list (shown from 800 px of module width, never scrolling: its rows are fitted to the height) to that class's names ranked by premium, each linking to its ticker page, and the same list ranks all classes by the chosen measure at rest. On a phone the long rows wrap to six pillars a line. The camera orbits by drag (yaw within 40 degrees, pitch 18 to 62), by Shift and the arrow keys, and Recentre returns to the opening view; the arrows alone, Home and End move a roving tab stop over the pillars (opening on the tallest), Enter selects and Escape clears. Labels go through one declutter pass and class names stagger to a lower line where neighbours would meet. Every colour is read from the page's tokens. No loop: a paint happens on mount, on data, on resize, on the switch, on hover, focus or selection and while dragging, and never at rest. The accessible twin is the canvas description, a hit button over each pillar with a full spoken sentence, and a dialog with the classes ranked by premium and the 40 largest names. A failed, pending or empty feed is a state with its reason. `tests/flows-market-floor-render.mjs` (Chromium, `page.route`, no server) recomputes every class sum, share, lean, height on the shared scale, row and column, and projection from the fixture rows with its own camera, in all three measures at 1440x820, 1024x768 and 390x844, holds labels for overlap and the canvas for the page, checks the readout, the side list, the tables, the keyboard, the orbit and its clamps, pixel colours, the dash for a class with no count, no universe, the failure states, a zero paint census at rest, the frame cost against a reference canvas workload, and fourteen planted wrong builds.

### `tests/flows-lean.mjs`

The one lean rule, held across the modules that derive it, with no server and no browser. A flagged window's premium is split into the dollars on the ask side, the bid side and neither (`FlowsUI.F.askSplit`); for a call the ask dollars are bullish and the bid dollars bearish, for a put the reverse, and the remainder is unsided, by convention. The suite loads `flows-ui.js` and the Floor, the River, the Network and Relations into a `vm` and runs each model on a known-answer table (a 510k against 490k call is +2% and not 100% bullish; a 300k, 100k and 600k-between call is +20%; the put mirror; a one-sided window; sides past the premium; no call or put) and on three seeded corpora of 300 windows, holding every tile, lane, name, network total and lean series to an independent sum, and every River sphere to the sphere rule the Now list uses (at least half the premium sided and at least 65% of the sided premium on one side). Thirteen planted wrong builds, a source scan that the ticker's alert card prints its ask side through `F.askSaid` with the sided share (the positioning builder and the dossier use the same rule, held by their own contracts), and a null calibration of the third Relations layer (K unrelated names must average r 0 and show no more than 7% of pairs, which the all-names factor fails at -1/(K-1)), among them the whole window added to its majority side, a put read as a call and the unsided premium counted as bullish, must each fail.

### `tests/flows-desk-ratio.mjs`, `tests/lib/pc-oracle.mjs`

Premium ÷ collateral per contract, the owner's requirement for the premium desk. The figure is the bid over the collateral of one contract in integer cents, half-up (`premiumLine` in `shared/flows-premium.js`, with a copy in `assets/js/flows-desk.js` that derives it from `r.bid` and `r.collateral` so a kept copy written before the field existed still shows it): premium is `roundCents(bid x 100)`, collateral is the strike x 100 for a cash-secured put, 100 shares at the price shown for a covered call and the convention's figure for a short call, all gross of the premium received except the Reg-T style figure, which is the requirement net of it. Every put, covered-call and short-call line prints the percentage as the first cell with both dollar amounts of one contract under it, visible at every width, muted when more than half the premium is in-the-money value; `rank=premiumRatio` sorts by it (labelled as counting in-the-money value, so deep in-the-money lines lead by design), `yieldOnCollateral` is relabelled Time value ÷ collateral, the frontier tooltip, the Best deployment card and its alternatives print it, and the CSV carries it as a fraction half-up to six decimals (ten for a figure under half a millionth, never zero). Percentages are written from integers (`1005/100000` is `1.01%`, `9995/100000` is `10.0%`, under 0.01% is `<0.01%`), never from `toFixed`. A missing bid or collateral is a dash with its reason. The forward fact says so when the chain's parity forward (five or more pairs) and the engine's forward put the delta more than 0.02 apart, the exact difference of the two N(d1) at the line's own mid volatility, and omits the sentence when there is no volatility. `tests/lib/pc-oracle.mjs` is the BigInt decimal reference. The suite is Chromium with `page.route` stubs, no server: the server and page against the reference over 20,000 random bids, the ranking fixture with a deep in-the-money put, the kept copy, the dashes, the tooltip, the plan, the export, the address, the sliced chain's ordering note, all four viewports for puts, calls and short calls, and planted wrong builds that each must fail. A scan reads every text node of the desk, hidden panels and popovers included, for null, undefined, NaN and [object across every side, rank and view at 1440 and 390, and a plan card that printed the word null (the DOM turns a null child into that string) is held by a planted build.

### `assets/js/flows-desk-calls.js`, `assets/css/flows-desk.css`, `tests/flows-desk-calls.mjs`, `tests/flows-desk-calls-render.mjs`, `tests/flows-desk-onescreen.mjs`, `tests/gen-shortcall-fixtures.py`, `tests/fixtures-shortcall.json`

The premium desk's Short calls view (`strategy=sc`, the fourth item of Sell) and the desk's one-screen stage. `assets/js/flows-desk-calls.js` is the deferred group `desk-calls` (`FlowsUI.deskCalls`, built on `FlowsUI.deskKit`, which `flows-desk.js` publishes and the chunk reads at call time): it prices the chain `strategy=cc` already fetches as uncovered short calls (the lab's `custom` family on the same smile and law, so Calls to Short calls makes no request), sized to N contracts (default 100 of 100 shares, a reading the page states) under a labelled collateral convention: delta-adjusted `100 x spot x max(h x absolute delta, f)` with h 30% and f 10% by default, a Reg-T style `100 x max(0.2 x spot - OTM, 0.1 x spot)`, or shares held (the covered call). Premium is the bid; the Ratio cell is Premium ÷ collateral, the whole bid over the collateral of one contract (in-the-money value included, both dollar amounts printed beside it), and the annualised ratio is time value over collateral on 365 days; the terms live in a Terms dialog and the address (`n`, `cv`, `h`, `f`, defaults omitted). The frontier plots the annualised ratio on a square-root scale against the magnitude of net delta or implied win %, with the floor guide at f over h and a diamond where the floor binds. Money is integer cents with half-up ties. The export follows OD-30 (no bid, ask, premium or spot column; its Premium ÷ collateral, Time value ÷ collateral and annualised columns are the site's own ratios). The pure oracle is `shortCallLine` and its helpers in `shared/flows-premium.js`. `assets/css/flows-desk.css` also holds the desk's one-screen stage: a tablist (Frontier, Lines, Smile, and Plan below 900 px) over a stage that fills the viewport, inactive panels `inert` and hidden in the same box, a Lines fitter with a Show all dialog, and a Filters dialog on a phone. `tests/gen-shortcall-fixtures.py` (Fractions) writes the cases of `tests/fixtures-shortcall.json`; `flows-desk-calls` holds the oracle and the chunk's copy to them in cents, 20,000 random draws to a BigInt reference, the engine ties and the planted wrong builds; `flows-desk-calls-render` holds every printed figure, the frontier's pixels, the popover formulas, the URL, the dialog, the export and the failure states; `flows-desk-onescreen` sweeps every state at 1440x820, 1024x768 and 390x844 for page scroll, clipping, inert panels and the Show all dialog, and fails six planted builds.

### `assets/js/flows-feed-os.js`, `tests/flows-feeds-onescreen.mjs`

The one-screen stage for Unusual and Events (`/flows/unusual/`, `/flows/events/`). `feedTabs` in `shared/flows-pages.js` renders a tablist over a `#fdStage` whose `.fd-mod` children are stacked and only the one with `data-on` is visible (the others `inert` and `visibility: hidden`), with `#view=<tab>` in the address; `.flows-main` is `100dvh` with no page scroll at 1440x820, 1024x768 and 390x844, laid out by `assets/css/flows-feeds.css`. `flows-feed-os.js` wires the tabs (arrow keys, Home, End) and a fitter: it unhides rows the painter hid behind its own disclosure, finds the vertically stacked group of repeated siblings in a module body that adds the most height, hides trailing rows until nothing overflows (`fd-cut`), then trailing legend and note lines, and puts a Show all button in the module header that opens the whole body in a dialog; it re-runs on mutation, resize and font load. The Flow network module is `data-free` (the fitter leaves it alone): its host fills the stage and nothing in it scrolls. `flows-unusual.js` mounts the 3D scene with `fit: true, barInHead: true` when the host is at least 640 by 300 px (the scene is laid out at the stage's own height, the legend row's height is reserved from the node scale so the two cannot feed back into each other, and Recentre, Freeze and Ambient sit in the module header), and the condensed map (`flows-net-map.js`, with its own sheet loaded on demand) below that, so the dock opening or a phone width changes the mode instead of cutting the scene. Events no longer names an Eastern date (the run's own session date), prints its build time in TRT, and writes `No print` and `No date` where a day has no macro release or FDA date, with a line naming the next macro print and FDA date beyond the five sessions. `tests/flows-feeds-onescreen.mjs` (Chromium, `page.route`, no server) sweeps every tab of both pages at the three sizes for page scroll, sideways scroll, clipped content, inert and hidden inactive modules, the address and the keyboard, opens each Show all dialog, holds the Events copy, and fails three planted wrong builds.

### `assets/css/flows-nn.css`, `assets/js/flows-nn-orb.js`

The Neuron identity (design v3 Lane 2). `flows-nn.css` holds the `--nn-*` tokens, the CSS orb (`.nn-orb`, the first paint and the fallback), the four-strip edge light (`.nn-edge`), the Ask screen edge `#nnScreen`, the wordmark, the app tile and the calc tile, the states (`idle`, `think`, `refresh`, `spoke`, `plain`, `off`, written as `data-nn` by `FlowsUI.nn`), motion only inside `prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference` with finite iteration counts, and the forced-colours block. The mark is the lens glyph (`g-neuron`, `g-neuron-s`; `g-calc` for arithmetic) and no mark carries four-fold rotational symmetry with curved arms. `assets/js/flows-nn-orb.js` is the deferred group `nn-orb` (`FlowsUI.nnOrb`: `mount`, `update`, `destroy`, `scan`): on every `.nn-orb` of 40 px or more outside `plain` and `off` it mounts a WebGL canvas that ray-traces a glass sphere per pixel (Schlick Fresnel, Snell's law with the indices 1.49, 1.51 and 1.53 for the three channels, eight-step emissive volumes from the Neuron tokens, a thin-film caustic along the upper third, an environment read once from the page's aurora and ground tokens). Idle is one frame; think and refresh run at most 30 frames a second, stop when the tab is hidden or the orb is off screen and after 180 s; spoke is one 720 ms bloom; reduced motion is one frame; no WebGL, a lost context, reduced transparency or forced colours leave the CSS orb. It is asked for by `flows-overview.js`, `flows-ticker.js` and `flows-oracle.js`.

### `assets/js/flows-oracle.js`, `assets/js/flows-oracle-core.js`, `assets/js/flows-oracle-boot.js`, `assets/css/flows-oracle.css`

The Neuron's always-on presence in the Flows shell (the client lane of the Oracle design; `FlowsUI.oracle`, `FlowsUI.oracleWords`, `FlowsUI.oracleLive` and `FlowsUI.oracleKit`, added to the existing global, no new one). `oracleShell(html, mode, slug)` in `shared/flows-pages.js` is the one server touch: for a member the switch allows (`FLOWS_ORACLE` is `on`, or `owner` for an owner; anything else is off and the page is byte for byte unchanged) it adds `<div id="fxOracle" hidden>`, two `<i hidden data-defer="oracle">` elements (the core, then the module; the stylesheet rides on the first as `data-css`), the 0.1 KB `flows-oracle-boot.js` that calls `FlowsUI.defer("oracle")` after the load event, a reserved 40 px `#orcHome` row on Home and a 40 px `#orcBand` row above the Unusual network, so nothing below them moves when they fill. The route `server/routes/flows-pages.js` wraps each rendered page in it. `flows-oracle-core.js` is pure: the wording tables (`STANCE_LINES`, 16 fixed strings, `MODEL_IMPLIED`, `callSay`, `liveSay`, each held to the Ask guard's forecast and modal lexicon and a 24-word sentence by `tests/flows-oracle-core.mjs`) and the seven live detectors `detect(state, rows, context, nowMs, {live, refuses})` over the flagged windows a page already holds (surge, same-strike repeat, lean flip by the site's convention, expiry cluster, sweep burst, size outlier, sector together), each compared with the name's own stored norm (a name with fewer than 20 windows of history or no stored context is silent), silent outside the regular session (Eastern arithmetic proven equal to the zone at every quarter hour of 2026), for the first 20 minutes, for a feed that is not live and for a fixture name, with a 30 minute cooldown per name and kind unless the strength rises, a cap of six messages in 30 minutes (the rest counted as quiet ones), an event multiplier of 1.5 inside 21 sessions of a report and dedupe by window id. `flows-oracle.js` reads `GET /api/flows/oracle?top=1` once, `?t=` for the ticker page and `?ts=` (at most 12, once in 30 s) for names the flow names that carry no context yet; a 404 or a failed read is the honest resting state with Retry, never an error wall. The orb is a 52 px glass disc (a button with `aria-haspopup="dialog"`, 44 px target) with states calm, watching (a turning arc), signal (a pill with the headline, a count badge; on a phone a toast for 6 s), conflict (a split rim) and stale (outlined); it opens a dialog sheet (380 px on a desktop, a 70 dvh bottom sheet on a phone) with the live messages, the name's record (stance chip with glyph and word, up to three call cards: cheap or rich badge with arrow, three tabular figures, `Show why` details on a grid-rows reveal, the model-implied line under every call) and the strongest names of the day; focus trap, `inert` page behind, Escape and scrim close and return focus to the orb, arrow keys move between cards, one polite announcement every five seconds. Motion is CSS only (the repository's spring tokens, `transform` and `opacity` and `clip-path`), none under reduced motion, no frame loop at rest. The preference is the first-party cookie `fn_oracle` (`on`, `quiet` or `off`, Path `/flows`; it can lower the page's mode, never raise it; Shift+N restores a hidden orb) and an optional `fn_oracle_haptic`. The ticker's reading rail gets a one-line `.orc-glance` button above its footer through `FlowsUI.oracle.glance(rail, ticker)`. It makes no vendor call and no model call, writes text and never markup, and names only TRT.

### `shared/flows-page-ledger.js`, `assets/js/flows-sfc.js`, `assets/css/flows-sfc.css`, `tests/flows-sfc-render.mjs`

The Premium ledger page, behind `FLOWS_LEDGER` (`wrangler.toml`, shipped `owner`: `on` opens it to members, `owner` to owners only, `off` closes it, anything else fails closed for a member; a closed page is the unknown-page 404 and the data route stays open to any signed-in member). `shared/flows-page-ledger.js` is the markup (title, period control, breadcrumb, check strip, the Ledger, Sectors and Reading panels, the info dialog written from the copy module) that `ledgerPage` in `shared/flows-pages.js` wraps; `assets/js/flows-sfc.js` reads `GET /api/flows/ledger` for the scope and period in the address (`#scope=sector%3AEnergy&period=session&w=flow`), refreshes on the heartbeat of `live:alerts`, `live:breadth` and `flowalerts`, and draws the 11 by 6 matrix in three column groups (one group at a time on a phone), the 13-row sector table with an inline tilt bar (one scale for every row printed in the head, an outlined band from R minus W to R plus W, a tick at R and a fill only where the verdict is proven, with a glyph and a word beside every verdict), the names of a sector and the windows of a name beneath it, and the flow view as arrows between takers and providers on one linear width scale; hovering or focusing a cell prints its exact figure under the matrix. It adds no global and makes no request but that one route. One screen at 1920x1080, 1920x940, 1440x820, 1024x768, 390x844 and 390x664 with no scroll; a phone shows one panel at a time behind three tabs, and on a screen under 721 px tall the flow view drops its node boxes into the band headings. `tests/flows-sfc-render.mjs` (Chromium, `page.route`, no server) holds the cells to the view, the displayed matrix to zero by rows and columns, the bars to independent geometry, the states, the keyboard, a touch layout, forced colors and axe, sets aside the rows that do not fit a short screen behind a Show all dialog that holds every row, with the total saying it counts all of them, and fails eighteen planted wrong builds of the served files.

### `tests/flows-layout-sweep.mjs`

The layout sweep over every Flows page function (eighteen specs) at 1920x1080, 1440x820, 1024x768 and 390x844 (`SIZES=1600x900,1280x720,2560x1440` adds odd sizes), each page's tabs and segmented controls visited in turn, in Chromium against `page.route` stubs and the pipeline's dry emit with no server. Per view it records page scroll and sideways scroll, inner scrollers, text clipped by its own box (a leaf whose scrollWidth or scrollHeight exceeds its client box with overflow hidden, ellipsis and line-clamp excepted), children that leave an overflow-hidden parent and canvases whose backing store has another aspect ratio than the box they are shown in. It fails on page or inner scroll of the one-screen pages, on clipped text, on a child that leaves an overflow-hidden parent and on any page error at the four standard sizes, and on any canvas mismatch; the only findings printed and not gated are the page scroll of pages that are not one screen and views at the odd sizes of `SIZES`. `REPORT_ONLY=1` never fails, `JSON=<file>` writes every view, `SHOTS=<dir>` saves a screenshot of each.

### `scripts/approval-scene.mjs`, `scripts/approval-worlds.mjs`, `scripts/approval-frames.mjs`, `scripts/approval-checks.mjs`, `tests/fixtures-approval/*`, `tests/approval-frames.mjs`

The approval render set of the Frontier programme (F-02): six placements (the Home network with the seam at the Name layer, the Moves page, the skyline, the terrain, the surface, Relations) rendered from fixtures at 1440x900 and 390x844 with `node scripts/render-preview.mjs --approval [--capture] --out <dir>`; `--capture` also records the 20-second Live and Ambient capture. The scene modules are pure (no DOM, no Node API) so the capture page inlines `approval-scene.mjs` and runs the same code in the browser. The fixtures are `placements.json` (what each placement is for and its use test), `world.json` (the sample worlds), `copy.json` (the five canonical strings, the Live and Ambient sentence, the seam sentence), `superseded-frames.json` (the 35 frames the 2026-10-05 19:20 direction replaced, by file name), `answers.json` (one slot per placement and OD-V1's, every value `null` until the owner answers) and `gates.json` (the rows that name OD-39 and the placements each waits for); `gateOf` resolves a row to `gated`, `changes` or `released` and a null answer is never read as a yes. `tests/approval-frames.mjs` (Chromium, no server) holds the DOM text lint (no "at ask" or "at bid", no "NOW", no FDR in a heading, no Unusual or Radar in a nav label or title, header sentence at most 14 words, chip at most 3), the canonical strings verbatim in the Moves frames, the Home network's draw list (no curve through a Name node, no pulse across two bands, conservation, one scale for sphere area and band width, no chip on a sphere), the skyline's label placer against its prisms, the terrain's signs, the surface's total variance, Relations' stability steps and the capture (Live still between arrivals, Ambient swaying), each with a negative fixture. Nothing under `assets/` or `shared/` is part of it.

### `docs/launch-checklist.md`, `scripts/launch-readiness.mjs`, `tests/launch-readiness.mjs`

The go/no-go list for the first paying member as a readback: ten lines in parts, each a repository check (config, schema, routes, source), a dated readback recorded in the checklist's table, or a part dropped by an owner decision. `node scripts/launch-readiness.mjs` prints which lines are true and exits 1 until all are. The test holds the checklist's parts table equal to the script's, every recorded row valid, and the admission rule: no paid surface (`shared/plans.js`, a `subscriptions` or `billing_events` table, a `/api/billing/` route, `FLOWS_BILLING_MODE` other than `off`) while a line is false.

## tests

Suites, helpers and fixtures.

### `tests/route-matrix.mjs`, `tests/fixtures-route-matrix.json`

The Worker's whole routing surface in process, with no server: every route literal and prefix family, as anonymous, Flows member, Flows owner and Lab user, under six methods (plus the rail with `PULSE` absent, the switch off and the rail on, and cross-origin and owner-mismatch mutations), on a pinned clock, a fake `ASSETS`, a refusing `fetch` and `tests/lib/d1-fake.mjs`. Status, `Allow`, `Content-Type`, `Cache-Control`, `Location`, `error.code`, the seven security headers, the `X-Fresh-*` set and the cookie names are compared with the snapshot; a behaviour change regenerates it with `node route-matrix.mjs --write` in a pull request that moves no code. The fake D1 cannot return `RETURNING` rows, so the `DELETE /api/progress` cell is a 500 on the fake only. It also holds `worker.js` to one top-level `const state = createState()` and no other top-level mutable state, and `server/` (when it exists) to none.

| File | Exports | Suites |
|---|---|---|
| `tests/fixtures-route-matrix.json` |  | route-matrix |
| `tests/route-matrix.mjs` |  | route-matrix |

### `tests/flows-engine-budget.mjs`, `tests/flows-d1-budget.mjs`, `tests/lib/d1-budget.mjs`

The engines' D1 budget as a contract, in rows, with no server. `tests/lib/d1-budget.mjs` holds the caps (100,000 rows written and 5,000,000 read a day on Workers Free), the cron firings expanded from `wrangler.toml` (a weekday is 108 Tier 1, 108 focus, 48 housekeeping and 48 summary firings, a weekend day the last two), the per-firing ceilings of each job, the Tier 2 pass, the nightly's keys, the rail's roster loads, the planned writers and readers with the plan row that builds each, and `D1_USERS`, the modules that prepare a statement. `flows-engine-budget` drives the real code on the counting fake: every one of a weekday's 312 `scheduled` firings through `worker.js` on one database with Tier 2 passes between them, one ingest of each nightly key class, the roster batch, and the focus tick's held-row query, which it also runs with counting SQL functions to settle the inference (SQLite visits each held name once and re-runs the inner field scan once per name, stopping at the px field: 44 elements against the fake's bound of 641 rows: 22 names and the 28 fields of a name's strip). The fake (`tests/lib/d1-fake.mjs`) now prices `json_each(payload, ...) AS r`, bare or aliased, over the rows a statement names, and a json_each nested in a correlated subquery once for each element of the scan it sits under. A ceiling must hold and must not be loose. `flows-d1-budget` sums every declared writer and reader against the caps (about 13,000 written and 336,000 read, with every firing counted as a cold start and the planned rows included) and fails when a module in the Worker's closure prepares a statement that no declaration prices. The observer (P1-25) is declared, not measured: its ceiling and `shared/flows-engine-verdicts.js` must arrive together. Whether D1 bills `json_each` elements and DDL as rows is not measurable on a local database, so both are counted.

### `tests/flows-ticker-firstpaint-render.mjs`

The ticker's first paint on the card alone. `start()` in `flows-ticker.js` paints as soon as the card is in, after a 250 ms grace for the two fast dossier routes (`card-x`, `hist`); a route that has not answered is drawn as loading, never as absent (the reading rail says `Loading the reading.`, the verdict strip and card `Loading the Neuron's read.`, the dossier modules give `Loading this name's dossier.` as their reason) and is applied in place when it lands: the summary through `renderVerdict` (the rail, and the reading and claims handed to the glance's `update()`) and `buildWorlds`, card-x and hist through `paintDossier`, which rebuilds the dossier modules and the dock and hands card-x to `update()` without remounting the glance chart, dropping the surface view or closing a module open in place. `tests/flows-ticker-firstpaint-render.mjs` (Chromium, `page.route` over the dry-run emit's AAPL card, no server) holds each of summary, card-x and hist behind a gate the test opens (every delay counts from the page's request for the tape route, which it makes once the card is accepted, and the grace case holds the page's 250 ms timer until the first paint, so no ordering rests on the card's transfer time or the machine's load): the page paints with the summary out, with the dossier out and with all three out, a dossier inside the grace is in the first paint, a late dossier keeps the mounted chart node and the open sheet, and a summary at 1.5 s and 5 s or all three late end in the baseline's modules, verdict, strip, claims and glance cards with no duplicate id. Seven planted wrong builds must fail: the first paint waits on the summary, no grace, a late summary never applied, a late dossier never applied, a late dossier stored but not repainted, a late dossier that remounts the page, and a rail that says there is no reading while the summary loads.

The flow network on the real unusual page in Chromium against `page.route` stubs, no server: layer counts and premium conservation through every layer, hop and node against an independent sum in Node, the ranking on screen at rest, at the reachable yaw and pitch limits, after reordering data and under reduced motion, Live at rest with a paint census of zero over 10 s, the arrival that lights only its own bands and expires after five minutes, the Ambient button, tag, legend, remembered choice and events, the owner default and the closed-session default, no `lighter` pass and no sway in Live, contracts in the tooltips, mouse and touch orbit (clamps at the angle where a label would collide, inertia, recentring, a vertical touch still scrolling the page), hit targets after a rotation, the painter's order, hover, row and keyboard highlighting, the table and the canvas label, the freshness pill, the Expiry output, a flare on a new window, focus kept through a rebuild, pause when hidden or off screen, reduced motion, the pending, empty, failed and unread states, no overflow, no label or caption collision and every label within half a pitch of its own sphere at 320, 390, 700, 768, 1024, 1280 and 1440 px in both outputs over a 9 by 5 yaw and pitch grid, along sixteen points of the sway path and where a long drag stops, and the v3 audit (`auditFn`, run in the page on `net.scene()`) at rest at 1440, 1024 and 390 (columns or rows equally spaced within 1 px, every stack centred on the midline within 1 px, chips on one x edge per column, sphere radius, band width and each node's outgoing slice sum on the stated scale, the same total on every header, the legend's reference circles and swatches on that scale) and over a 0 to 40 s sweep at 0.1 s (no chip over a sphere or a chip, nothing outside the canvas); a readout and header clearance audit (a hover readout at least 12 px clear of every header and inside the canvas) and eight mutants of the served module (equal spheres, bands not by premium, unequal columns, an off-centre stack, a ragged chip edge, a header with another total, headers up under the readout, headers down on the spheres) that the audit must fail; a delayed-font block at 320 and 700 px (the woff2 held back 3 s, then labels, layout and collisions re-checked once it lands); at 320 and 390 px the legend's three circles on one row and its three swatches on one row, and no table header with a scrollWidth over its clientWidth; breathing between 1.00 and 1.03, Freeze, `destroy()` leaving no observer or listener of its own, no retained heap after 3,000 frames and under 3 KiB allocated a frame; CPU per frame at 120 or more bands and 200 pulses, also with the raster forced to finish (a pixel read after every draw), held as a ratio to a reference canvas workload timed in the same interleaved windows rather than as milliseconds, since an absolute figure flaked at load 10 on identical code (limit 2.5 times the reference, floor 12 ms; 1.4 to 1.5 measured). `NET_SHOTS=<dir>` saves screenshots.
